# Clasptek Prep Portal Version 2

## Phase 0.5 — Canonical Domain, Business Rules & Logical Data Design

**Document status:** Architecture Approval Draft  
**Authority:** This specification governs all Phase 1+ implementation unless superseded by an approved Architecture Decision Record.  
**Scope:** Canonical logical design only. It contains no SQL, application implementation, frontend design or vendor-specific physical schema.  
**Planning horizon:** 5–10 years  
**Architecture styles:** Domain-Driven Design, Clean Architecture, modular monolith with asynchronous workers, enterprise integration patterns, event-driven projections and database-first logical governance.

## 0. Purpose, Authority and Governing Principles

This document converts the approved product and enterprise architecture into canonical logical models. It is the single reference for domain ownership, business objects, invariants, lifecycle transitions, commands, events, data relationships, permissions, security boundaries, versioning, migration and the Phase 1 readiness decision.

### Canonical Outcome Flow

```text
Student Relationship
→ Enrollment
→ Exam Goal
→ Preparation Journey
→ Diagnostic Baseline
→ Competency Gap
→ Learning Path
→ Study Plan
→ Learning Workspace
→ Learning / Adaptive Practice / Assessment / Exam Simulation
→ Academic Evidence
→ Mastery
→ Exam Readiness
→ Recommendation
→ Student Success Intervention where needed
→ Official Result
→ Target Outcome
```

### Non-negotiable distinctions

1. **Student Relationship** describes the learner’s relationship with an academy; it does not grant course access.
2. **Enrollment** grants course-offering entitlement; it does not measure academic readiness.
3. **Course** organizes content and preparation capabilities; it is not the student’s journey.
4. **Preparation Journey** coordinates one learner’s effort toward one primary exam objective.
5. **Learning Workspace** is a personalized read/orchestration capability; it is not a source-of-truth domain.
6. **Academic Evidence** records traceable observations; **Mastery** interprets competency ability; **Readiness** predicts target achievement.
7. **Practice, assessment, diagnostic and simulation** share a delivery kernel but keep distinct academic policies.
8. **Analytics and workspace projections** are rebuildable; they never override transactional truth.
9. **Published academic content is immutable.**
10. **AI output is advisory or provisional until governed rules determine its authority.**

### Approval status legend

- **Approval Draft:** logically complete but awaiting accountable owner sign-off.
- **Approved:** binding for physical design and implementation.
- **Superseded:** replaced only by an approved later version/ADR.

### Conformance rule

Every future database entity, application module, API contract, event, screen and background job must trace to an owner domain, aggregate, business rule and permission in this document. New concepts require an ADR before implementation.

# SECTION 1 — CANONICAL BUSINESS DOMAIN MODEL

The platform is divided into bounded contexts based on business ownership, consistency needs and change cadence. A domain owns its invariants and exposes commands, queries and events. Other domains must not update its records directly.

## 1.2 Domain classification

| Domain class             | Domains                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Architectural meaning                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Core differentiating     | Preparation Journey; Diagnostic Assessment; Competency Framework; Learning Path; Adaptive Practice; Exam Simulation; Academic Evidence; Mastery; Exam Readiness; Recommendation Engine; AI Academic Intelligence; Student Success; Academic Intelligence            | Directly differentiates Clasptek as an examination-preparation platform. These receive strongest domain modelling and product investment. |
| Supporting academic      | Exam Product & Specification; Course & Offering; Learning Content; File & Media; Question Bank; Assessment Definition; Examination Delivery; Submission & Grading; Psychometrics & Item Quality; Academic Publishing; Academic Operations; Achievement & Milestones | Required to produce and operate reliable preparation experiences.                                                                         |
| Generic/platform         | Identity & Access; Academy; Sponsor Relationship; Communication; Audit & Compliance; Integration; Platform Operations                                                                                                                                               | Necessary but should use standard patterns and avoid absorbing academic rules.                                                            |
| Experience/orchestration | Learning Workspace; Study Planning                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Compose capabilities without owning the authoritative academic facts they display.                                                        |

## 1.3 Ownership rule

A domain may read another domain through a published contract or event projection, but only the owning domain may execute invariant-changing commands. Direct cross-domain table writes are forbidden. Where one business transaction spans domains, the initiating domain commits its own state and a transactional outbox event; downstream domains process the event idempotently.

## 2.1 Directed dependency principle

Dependencies point from volatile experience and orchestration modules toward stable domain contracts, never toward another domain’s persistence implementation. Core academic domains publish facts; projections and operations consume them. Cycles are broken with events and read models.

## 2.2 Shared-kernel candidates

A shared kernel is justified only where identical semantics and transaction rules are mandatory:

1. **Examination Delivery Kernel:** Attempt, Response, authoritative timer, autosave sequencing, final submission and attempt integrity. Used by diagnostic, practice, assessment and simulation.
2. **Academic Version Identity:** stable identity, immutable version, effective period, supersession and publication manifest semantics.
3. **Actor and Scope Context:** principal, academy, assignment, enrollment/journey and correlation identifiers.
4. **Score Representation:** score scale, dimensions, ranges and conversion references. Calculation remains with the owning grading/readiness domain.
5. **Event Envelope:** event identity/version, correlation, causation, aggregate identity/version, academy, actor, occurrence/recording times and sensitivity.
6. **File Reference Contract:** opaque asset identity, version, sensitivity, scan state and authorized-delivery semantics.

The shared kernel must remain small. Course rules, readiness calculations, recommendation ranking, publication workflows and exam-specific behaviour are not shared-kernel concerns.

## 2.3 Forbidden dependencies

- UI or BFF → direct answer-definition storage.
- Learning Workspace → writes to transactional aggregates.
- Readiness → direct modification of grades, mastery evidence or goals.
- Recommendation Engine → direct completion of activities or access grants.
- AI provider adapters → direct database writes outside the AI orchestration boundary.
- Academic Intelligence → corrections to transactional records.
- Communication → business-state transitions.
- Instructor assignment → implied access outside explicit academy/course/offering scope.
- Exam-specific modules → new duplicate attempt, response, result or enrollment stores.
- Analytics warehouse → authorization source of truth.

## 2.4 Anti-corruption boundaries

External identity, AI, messaging, storage, official-result verification, proctoring, content/QTI, calendar and analytics providers must be translated into canonical Clasptek contracts. Provider identifiers and payloads are retained only in Integration and governed raw-output records; they must not leak into core domain vocabulary.

# SECTION 3 — CANONICAL BUSINESS OBJECT MODEL

Objects are grouped into aggregates. An aggregate root is the only entry point for invariant-changing commands within its consistency boundary. References across aggregates use identities and published contracts rather than in-memory object graphs.

## 3.3 Aggregate size rule

Large collections such as all responses, all question versions, all evidence or all timeline events must not be loaded as one aggregate graph. Aggregate boundaries enforce transactional consistency, not navigational convenience. Collections are queried through paginated projections and commands target specific child identities.

# SECTION 4 — BUSINESS RULE CATALOGUE

The catalogue contains **270 canonical logical rules**. Priorities mean:

- **Critical:** violation can compromise security, academic validity, tenant isolation, result reproducibility or core data integrity.
- **High:** violation materially damages product correctness, operations or student outcomes.
- **Medium:** violation creates inconsistency, avoidable debt or degraded experience.

Rules are implementation-neutral. Each must be traceable to automated tests, database/application constraints, operational controls or governance procedures.

| Rule ID | Category            | Description                                                                                                             | Reason                                                                                                                          | Priority | Affected Domains                                                                                            | Expected Behaviour                                                                                                |
| ------- | ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| IDN-001 | Identity            | A person must have one canonical profile even when holding multiple roles or academy memberships.                       | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Identity reconciliation must link provider identities to one canonical person or open a controlled merge review.  |
| IDN-002 | Identity            | Authentication identity and application profile must remain separate records.                                           | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Authentication changes must not overwrite academic or relationship data.                                          |
| IDN-003 | Identity            | Human users must never share an account.                                                                                | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | The system must reject or investigate shared-account patterns and require individual identities.                  |
| IDN-004 | Identity            | Email or equivalent identity verification is required before privileged or assessment access.                           | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Unverified identities may finish onboarding but cannot receive protected entitlements.                            |
| IDN-005 | Identity            | Multi-factor authentication is mandatory for privileged staff roles.                                                    | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Privileged access must be blocked until an approved second factor is enrolled.                                    |
| IDN-006 | Identity            | Sessions must be revocable by the user and authorized administrators.                                                   | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Revocation must invalidate future requests and record a security event.                                           |
| IDN-007 | Identity            | Account suspension must not delete academic history.                                                                    | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Suspension blocks authentication while preserving enrollments, attempts, grades and audit evidence.               |
| IDN-008 | Identity            | Roles must be assigned through scoped RoleAssignment records rather than a single profile field.                        | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Authorization must evaluate active role, academy and resource scope.                                              |
| IDN-009 | Identity            | A person may hold multiple non-conflicting roles.                                                                       | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | The system must combine only permissions valid in the current scope and enforce separation-of-duties conflicts.   |
| IDN-010 | Identity            | Sponsor access must use a sponsor identity and relationship, never the student's credentials.                           | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Sponsor actions must be attributable to the sponsor and limited by consent.                                       |
| IDN-011 | Identity            | Service identities must be distinguishable from human identities.                                                       | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Automated actions must carry service identity, purpose and credential rotation metadata.                          |
| IDN-012 | Identity            | Recovery flows must apply rate limits and verified channels.                                                            | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | Critical | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Repeated or suspicious recovery attempts must be throttled and audited.                                           |
| IDN-013 | Identity            | Identity merge must be a controlled, reversible administrative process.                                                 | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Duplicate profiles must not be silently merged; source identifiers and affected relationships must be reconciled. |
| IDN-014 | Identity            | Account closure requests must respect legal and academic retention obligations.                                         | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Personal data eligible for erasure must be separated from records that must be retained or de-identified.         |
| IDN-015 | Identity            | Security-sensitive profile changes must notify the affected user.                                                       | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | Email, phone, MFA or password changes must trigger a non-suppressible security notice.                            |
| IDN-016 | Identity            | Risk-based throttling must apply to login, registration and verification attempts.                                      | Protect identity integrity, scoped access and traceable responsibility.                                                         | High     | Identity & Access; Student Relationship; Academy                                                            | The platform must limit abuse without locking out legitimate users indefinitely.                                  |

| RDY-009 | Readiness           | Weak and strong skills must map to versioned competencies.                                                              | Measure likely target achievement from product-specific, recent and reliable evidence rather than completion.                   | High     | Exam Readiness; Mastery; Goal Management; Academic Evidence; Exam Simulation                                | Free-text observations may supplement but not replace canonical skill outputs.                                    |
| RDY-010 | Readiness           | Readiness recalculation must be event-driven and idempotent.                                                            | Measure likely target achievement from product-specific, recent and reliable evidence rather than completion.                   | High     | Exam Readiness; Mastery; Goal Management; Academic Evidence; Exam Simulation                                | Duplicate evidence events do not create conflicting snapshots.                                                    |
| RDY-011 | Readiness           | Readiness snapshots must be immutable.                                                                                  | Measure likely target achievement from product-specific, recent and reliable evidence rather than completion.                   | Critical | Exam Readiness; Mastery; Goal Management; Academic Evidence; Exam Simulation                                | A new snapshot supersedes rather than overwrites history.                                                         |

| RDY-013 | Readiness           | Invalidated evidence must trigger recalculation.                                                                        | Measure likely target achievement from product-specific, recent and reliable evidence rather than completion.                   | Critical | Exam Readiness; Mastery; Goal Management; Academic Evidence; Exam Simulation                                | Affected mastery and readiness are rebuilt from the valid evidence boundary.                                      |
| RDY-014 | Readiness           | Target-ready status requires policy thresholds for score, confidence and coverage.                                      | Measure likely target achievement from product-specific, recent and reliable evidence rather than completion.                   | Critical | Exam Readiness; Mastery; Goal Management; Academic Evidence; Exam Simulation                                | A strong single-section score cannot unlock full readiness.                                                       |

| SEC-001 | Security            | Every academy-owned record must carry or derive an unambiguous academy boundary.                                        | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Queries and commands fail closed when scope cannot be resolved.                                                   |
| SEC-002 | Security            | Row-level security must default to deny for protected data.                                                             | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Access exists only through explicit policies and service pathways.                                                |
| SEC-003 | Security            | Application authorization and database policy must both protect sensitive workflows.                                    | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Neither layer is treated as the sole control.                                                                     |
| SEC-004 | Security            | Service-role credentials must never be exposed to client applications.                                                  | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Privileged operations execute only in trusted server/worker environments.                                         |
| SEC-005 | Security            | Answer definitions must be isolated from student-readable contracts.                                                    | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Negative tests verify that identifiers, joins and storage paths cannot reveal answers.                            |
| SEC-006 | Security            | Object storage for student work and premium content must be private.                                                    | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Access uses short-lived authorized delivery.                                                                      |
| SEC-007 | Security            | File uploads must be validated, scanned and quarantined before active use.                                              | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Unsafe or mismatched files remain inaccessible.                                                                   |
| SEC-008 | Security            | Sensitive exports require explicit permission, purpose and audit.                                                       | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Large or restricted exports may require approval and asynchronous delivery.                                       |
| SEC-009 | Security            | Privileged changes must require reauthentication where risk warrants it.                                                | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | High     | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Role, grade, timer, security and destructive actions may prompt step-up authentication.                           |

| SEC-011 | Security            | Secrets must be stored in a managed secret system and rotated.                                                          | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | No secret appears in source, logs, prompts or client bundles.                                                     |
| SEC-012 | Security            | Logs must exclude tokens, answer keys and full restricted submissions.                                                  | Protect tenant isolation, examination integrity, personal data and privileged operations.                                       | Critical | Identity & Access; Audit & Compliance; Platform Operations; all academy-owned domains                       | Structured redaction applies before persistence.                                                                  |

| ADM-017 | Administration      | Support impersonation must be visible and fully audited.                                                                | Govern academic quality and sensitive operations beyond ordinary record management.                                             | Critical | Academic Operations; Academic Publishing; Academy; Audit & Compliance                                       | The original actor and reason remain attached to every action.                                                    |

# SECTION 5 — STATE MACHINES

State is changed only through named commands. Every transition validates the current aggregate version, actor scope and guard conditions. Unsupported transitions fail without partial writes. Time-driven transitions are performed by an authorized scheduler/service and remain auditable.

## 5.1 Student Relationship

| From State                         | Command / Trigger             | To State                        | Guard / Preconditions                                   |
| ---------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| prospect                           | RegisterStudent               | applicant                       | Required identity/contact fields valid                  |
| applicant                          | CompleteRegistration          | registered                      | Identity verified and profile minimum complete          |
| registered                         | ActivateStudentRelationship   | active_student                  | Academy accepts relationship or enrollment activates    |
| active_student                     | DeactivateStudentRelationship | inactive_student                | No active entitlement or explicit administrative action |
| inactive_student                   | ReactivateStudentRelationship | active_student                  | Eligibility and account security valid                  |
| active_student                     | MarkAlumnus                   | alumnus                         | No active preparation and academy alumni criteria met   |
| alumnus                            | ReengageStudent               | active_student                  | New enrollment or reactivation approved                 |
| any_non_archived                   | SuspendStudentRelationship    | suspended                       | Security, conduct or administrative policy              |
| suspended                          | ReinstateStudentRelationship  | active_student/inactive_student | Suspension resolved                                     |
| inactive_student/alumnus/suspended | ArchiveStudentRelationship    | archived                        | Retention and dependency checks pass                    |

## 5.2 Enrollment

| From State                  | Command / Trigger      | To State                          | Guard / Preconditions                                                        |
| --------------------------- | ---------------------- | --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| pending                     | ApproveEnrollment      | approved                          | Student, offering, capacity and commercial/administrative requirements valid |
| approved                    | ActivateEnrollment     | active                            | Access dates valid and offering eligible                                     |
| pending/approved            | CancelEnrollment       | cancelled                         | No protected activity requiring withdrawal path                              |
| active                      | SuspendEnrollment      | suspended                         | Authorized reason recorded                                                   |
| suspended                   | ResumeEnrollment       | active                            | Eligibility and time window valid                                            |
| active                      | CompleteEnrollment     | completed                         | Completion policy satisfied or authorized completion                         |
| active/suspended            | WithdrawEnrollment     | withdrawn                         | Reason and effective date recorded                                           |
| active/suspended            | ExpireEnrollment       | expired                           | Server time beyond access end or policy trigger                              |
| active/suspended            | TransferEnrollment     | transferred                       | Target offering approved and lineage created                                 |
| expired/withdrawn/completed | CreateRetakeEnrollment | terminal + new pending enrollment | Retake policy and target offering valid                                      |

## 5.3 Preparation Journey

| From State               | Command / Trigger            | To State                              | Guard / Preconditions                           |
| ------------------------ | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| goal_definition_pending  | GoalVersionActivated (event) | baseline_pending                      | Active goal version exists                      |
| baseline_pending         | DiagnosticAssigned (event)   | diagnostic_in_progress                | Diagnostic required and available               |
| baseline_pending         | AcceptPriorBaseline          | path_generation_pending               | Approved prior evidence meets waiver policy     |
| diagnostic_in_progress   | CompleteDiagnostic           | path_generation_pending               | Valid diagnostic evidence and report generated  |
| path_generation_pending  | AssignLearningPath           | foundation_building/skill_development | Path entry classification approved              |
| foundation_building      | AdvanceJourneyStage          | skill_development                     | Foundation exit criteria satisfied              |
| skill_development        | AdvanceJourneyStage          | exam_focused_preparation              | Competency and activity thresholds satisfied    |
| exam_focused_preparation | ReadinessUpdated (event)     | approaching_target                    | Current readiness meets approach threshold      |
| approaching_target       | ReadinessUpdated (event)     | target_ready                          | Score, confidence and coverage thresholds met   |
| target_ready             | ScheduleOfficialExam         | exam_scheduled                        | Exam date recorded                              |
| exam_scheduled           | RecordExamTaken              | exam_taken                            | Exam occurrence confirmed                       |
| exam_taken               | AwaitOfficialResult          | result_pending                        | Result not yet available                        |
| result_pending           | RecordOfficialResult         | outcome_review                        | Official result submitted/verified per policy   |
| outcome_review           | CompleteJourney              | completed                             | Outcome reviewed and closure decision recorded  |
| outcome_review           | PlanRetake                   | retake_planned                        | Target not achieved or new target set           |
| any_active               | PauseJourney                 | paused                                | Reason and resumption plan recorded             |
| paused                   | ResumeJourney                | previous_or_recalculated_stage        | Goal, enrollment and plan remain valid          |
| any_active               | MarkJourneyAtRisk            | at_risk                               | Explainable risk trigger exists                 |
| at_risk                  | ResolveJourneyRisk           | previous_or_recalculated_stage        | Intervention outcome and recalculation complete |
| any_non_terminal         | AbandonJourney               | abandoned                             | Authorized closure reason recorded              |

## 5.4 Goal

| From State          | Command / Trigger | To State                        | Guard / Preconditions                                      |
| ------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| draft               | ActivateGoal      | active                          | Exam product, score scale and target values valid          |
| active              | ReviseGoal        | superseded + new active version | Material change valid and impact analysis queued           |
| active              | PauseGoal         | paused                          | Journey treatment defined                                  |
| paused              | ResumeGoal        | active                          | Target remains feasible or revised                         |
| active              | MarkGoalAchieved  | achieved                        | Verified result or approved target evidence satisfies goal |
| active              | MarkGoalMissed    | missed                          | Outcome evaluated below defined target                     |
| draft/active/paused | CancelGoal        | cancelled                       | Reason and journey disposition recorded                    |

## 5.5 Learning Path

| From State             | Command / Trigger    | To State                         | Guard / Preconditions                           |

| generating             | ProposeLearningPath  | proposed                         | Baseline, goal and curriculum resolved          |
| proposed               | ApproveLearningPath  | active                           | Academic rules and access checks pass           |
| active                 | PauseLearningPath    | paused                           | Reason recorded                                 |
| paused                 | ResumeLearningPath   | active                           | Path remains compatible or revised              |
| active                 | ReviseLearningPath   | superseded + new active revision | Material evidence/goal/curriculum change        |
| active                 | CompleteLearningPath | completed                        | Required steps complete or approved equivalence |
| proposed/active/paused | CancelLearningPath   | cancelled                        | Journey closure or replacement path exists      |

| academic_review   | ApproveAcademicReview     | quality_assurance      | Academic accuracy and alignment pass                  |

| approval          | ApproveAssessment         | approved               | Authorized approver accepts package                   |
| approved          | PublishAssessment         | published              | Effective date, dependencies and form validation pass |
| published         | RetireAssessment          | retired                | No new deliveries; history preserved                  |
| published         | WithdrawAssessment        | withdrawn              | Critical defect and impact workflow opened            |
| retired/withdrawn | ArchiveAssessment         | archived               | Retention and operational checks pass                 |

| fidelity_review       | ApproveSimulationFidelity         | quality_assurance      | Exam behaviour matches specification                  |
| quality_assurance     | PassSimulationQA                  | pilot_testing/approval | Timing, media, tools, routing and recovery pass       |
| pilot_testing         | CompleteSimulationPilot           | approval               | Pilot thresholds met                                  |
| approval              | ApproveSimulation                 | approved               | Academic and operational approval                     |
| approved              | PublishSimulation                 | published              | Dependencies effective and capacity policy configured |

| scheduled             | OpenSimulation                    | open                   | Start time reached or authorized open                 |

| grading               | ReleaseSimulationResult           | released               | Effective grade and incident review complete          |

| active                | InvalidateSimulationSession       | invalidated            | Integrity/technical incident decision                 |

## 5.8 Submission

| From State                        | Command / Trigger         | To State       | Guard / Preconditions                                   |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------- | -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| draft                             | SaveSubmissionDraft       | draft          | Authorized owner and valid format                       |
| draft                             | SubmitWork                | received       | Idempotency, eligibility and content validation pass    |
| received                          | ValidateSubmission        | validated      | Asset scan, type, ownership and completeness pass       |

| queued                            | StartGrading              | grading        | Worker obtains lease and approved versions              |
| grading                           | CompleteGrading           | graded         | Structured output or human result validates             |

| finalized                         | ReleaseSubmissionFeedback | released       | Release policy satisfied                                |

## 5.9 AI Review

| From State        | Command / Trigger              | To State        | Guard / Preconditions                                  |
| ----------------- | ------------------------------ | --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| queued            | StartAIRun                     | running         | Approved model, prompt, rubric and context available   |
| running           | CompleteAIRun                  | output_received | Provider response received                             |
| output_received   | ValidateAIOutput               | validated       | Schema, score range, provenance and safety checks pass |
| output_received   | RejectAIOutput                 | failed          | Validation fails and retry policy exhausted            |
| validated         | AutoAcceptAIResult             | accepted        | Confidence and policy thresholds met                   |
| validated         | RouteToHumanReview             | review_required | Low confidence, disagreement, flag or policy           |
| review_required   | AssignReviewer                 | in_human_review | Authorized reviewer available                          |
| in_human_review   | ConfirmAIResult                | accepted        | Reviewer confirms                                      |
| in_human_review   | AdjustAIResult                 | adjusted        | Reviewer records criterion changes and reason          |
| in_human_review   | EscalateAIReview               | escalated       | Specialist review required                             |
| accepted/adjusted | GradeDecisionFinalized (event) | completed       | Effective grade decision created                       |
| failed            | RetryAIRun                     | queued          | Retry budget and failure class permit                  |
| any_non_terminal  | SuspendAIReview                | suspended       | Provider/model incident or policy hold                 |

## 5.10 Recommendation

| From State         | Command / Trigger                 | To State   | Guard / Preconditions                                    |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------- | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| generated          | ValidateRecommendationEligibility | eligible   | Activity accessible, released and appropriate            |
| generated          | RejectRecommendation              | rejected   | Candidate fails eligibility or policy                    |
| eligible           | PresentRecommendation             | presented  | Workspace and audience valid                             |
| presented          | AcceptRecommendation              | accepted   | Student or authorized staff accepts                      |
| presented          | DismissRecommendation             | dismissed  | Dismissal allowed and reason captured where needed       |
| presented/accepted | ExpireRecommendation              | expired    | Time, activity or evidence context becomes stale         |
| accepted           | StartRecommendedActivity          | acted_on   | Linked activity starts                                   |
| acted_on           | CompleteRecommendedActivity       | completed  | Verified activity completion                             |
| completed          | EvaluateRecommendationOutcome     | evaluated  | Outcome window and evidence available                    |
| presented/accepted | SupersedeRecommendation           | superseded | New policy/evidence creates more relevant recommendation |

## 5.11 Official Result

| From State                   | Command / Trigger         | To State                 | Guard / Preconditions                               |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| draft                        | SubmitOfficialResult      | submitted                | Product, date and score fields valid                |
| submitted                    | RequestResultVerification | verification_pending     | Verification policy requires evidence               |
| submitted                    | AcceptSelfReportedResult  | verified                 | Policy permits self-report with explicit provenance |
| verification_pending         | VerifyOfficialResult      | verified                 | Authorized verifier and evidence pass               |
| verification_pending         | RejectOfficialResult      | rejected                 | Evidence invalid or inconsistent                    |
| verified                     | EvaluateTargetOutcome     | evaluated                | Active goal version and score scale resolved        |
| evaluated                    | ArchiveOfficialResult     | archived                 | Outcome workflow complete and retention starts      |
| submitted/verified/evaluated | CorrectOfficialResult     | superseded + new version | Authorized correction with reason                   |

## 5.12 Achievement

| From State    | Command / Trigger             | To State      | Guard / Preconditions                              |
| ------------- | ----------------------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------- |
| draft         | ApproveAchievementDefinition  | approved      | Criteria measurable, safe and non-manipulative     |
| approved      | ActivateAchievementDefinition | active        | Effective period and event mappings valid          |
| active        | DetectQualifyingMilestone     | pending_award | Verified events satisfy criteria                   |
| pending_award | AwardAchievement              | awarded       | Uniqueness and eligibility pass                    |
| awarded       | RevokeAchievement             | revoked       | Underlying evidence invalidated or award erroneous |
| active        | RetireAchievementDefinition   | retired       | No new awards; history retained                    |

## 5.13 Question Lifecycle

| From State        | Command / Trigger               | To State               | Guard / Preconditions                                         |
| ----------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| draft             | SubmitQuestionForInternalReview | internal_review        | Prompt, response type and metadata complete                   |
| internal_review   | PassQuestionInternalReview      | academic_review        | Structural and duplicate checks pass                          |
| academic_review   | ApproveQuestionAcademically     | quality_assurance      | Accuracy, answer, explanation, competency and difficulty pass |
| quality_assurance | PassQuestionQA                  | pilot_testing/approval | Rendering, accessibility, media and answer isolation pass     |
| pilot_testing     | CompleteQuestionPilot           | approval               | Pilot data and issue resolution acceptable                    |
| approval          | ApproveQuestionVersion          | approved               | Authorized approver accepts version                           |
| approved          | PublishQuestionVersion          | published              | Package dependencies approved                                 |
| published         | RetireQuestionVersion           | retired                | No new ordinary use; history preserved                        |
| published         | WithdrawQuestionVersion         | withdrawn              | Critical defect/exposure with impact analysis                 |
| retired/withdrawn | ArchiveQuestionVersion          | archived               | Retention checks pass                                         |

## 5.14 Publishing Lifecycle

| From State        | Command / Trigger         | To State               | Guard / Preconditions                                         |
| ----------------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| draft             | SubmitForInternalReview   | internal_review        | Publication candidate complete                                |
| internal_review   | CompleteInternalReview    | academic_review        | Structure and metadata pass                                   |
| academic_review   | CompleteAcademicReview    | quality_assurance      | Academic accuracy and alignment pass                          |
| quality_assurance | CompleteQualityAssurance  | pilot_testing/approval | Technical, accessibility, security and dependency checks pass |
| pilot_testing     | CompletePilotTesting      | approval               | Pilot criteria pass or approved tier waiver                   |
| approval          | ApprovePublicationPackage | approved               | Required approvers and separation of duties satisfied         |
| approved          | SchedulePublication       | scheduled              | Effective date and dependencies valid                         |
| scheduled         | PublishPackage            | published              | Activation transaction succeeds                               |
| published         | RetirePackage             | retired                | Normal end-of-life                                            |
| published         | WithdrawPackage           | withdrawn              | Critical emergency action with impact workflow                |
| retired/withdrawn | ArchivePackage            | archived               | Retention and operational dependency checks pass              |

## 5.15 Common state-machine rules

1. States use stable canonical codes; UI labels may vary by product or locale.
2. Terminal records remain historically resolvable.
3. Corrections use supersession or explicit invalidation rather than silent reversal.
4. Every consequential transition records actor/service, reason where required, source state, target state, occurred time, aggregate version and correlation identifier.
5. State transitions are optimistic-concurrency controlled.
6. External retries use idempotency keys.
7. A projection may lag; command validation always uses the owning aggregate’s current state.

# SECTION 6 — DOMAIN EVENTS

The enterprise event catalogue contains **188 event types** derived from domain ownership. Events describe completed business facts in past tense. They are not commands.

### Canonical event envelope

Every event carries: event identifier, event type and version, occurred and recorded timestamps, producer, aggregate type/identifier/version, academy scope where applicable, actor or service identity, correlation and causation identifiers, sensitivity classification, and a versioned payload.

Delivery is at least once. Consumers must be idempotent. Event order is guaranteed only where explicitly required for the same aggregate/partition.

## 6.1 Event compatibility

- Additive optional payload fields are backward compatible.
- Removing or changing meaning requires a new event version.
- Consumers ignore unknown optional fields.
- Sensitive fields are minimized; full essays, recordings, answer definitions and secrets are referenced, not embedded.
- Outbox publication occurs in the same transaction as the producer’s state change.
- Dead-letter handling never silently discards an academic or security event.

# SECTION 7 — COMMAND MODEL

The command catalogue contains **171 canonical commands**. Commands express intent and may be rejected. They are handled by the domain that owns the target aggregate.

Every command envelope includes command identifier, command type/version, actor/service identity, academy/scope context, target aggregate identifier/version where applicable, idempotency key for repeatable external requests, requested time, correlation and causation identifiers, and validated payload.

## 7.1 Command handling rules

1. Commands are accepted only by the owner domain.
2. Validation order is: authentication → academy/scope → permission → aggregate existence/version → state → references → domain invariants → quota/policy.
3. Rejections use stable business error codes and cause no partial state.
4. Commands that create external side effects commit an outbox event first; workers perform side effects asynchronously.
5. Bulk commands decompose into individually validated results unless the business explicitly requires all-or-nothing atomicity.
6. Commands never accept arbitrary status fields; callers request transitions by intent.
7. Read models cannot handle commands.

# SECTION 8 — LOGICAL DATA MODEL

The logical model is relational and normalized for transactional truth. Structured data is permitted only for bounded, validated variation such as exam policies, response payloads and provider metadata. Identifiers are opaque. All academy-owned entities carry or deterministically derive academy scope.

## 8.1 Logical context groups

| Context group               | Principal entities                                                                                                                                                         | Authoritative concern                                                       |
| --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Identity and relationship   | UserAccount; PersonProfile; StudentRelationship; Academy; AcademyMembership; RoleAssignment; SponsorRelationship                                                           | Who the actor is, their institutional relationship and scoped authority     |
| Examination definition      | ExamProduct; ExamProductVersion; ScoreScale; CompetencyFrameworkVersion; Competency; CompetencyMapping                                                                     | What examination is being prepared for and how abilities/scores are defined |
| Course entitlement          | Course; CourseVersion; CourseOffering; Cohort; InstructorAssignment; Enrollment; EnrollmentTransition                                                                      | What academic product is available and who is entitled to it                |
| Preparation orchestration   | ExamGoal; GoalVersion; PreparationJourney; JourneySnapshot; DiagnosticAssignment/Report; LearningPath; StudyPlan                                                           | Why, where and how the learner is preparing                                 |
| Academic content            | CurriculumVersion; Module; Lesson; LearningActivity; FileAsset                                                                                                             | What instruction and resources are available                                |
| Item and publication        | QuestionItem; QuestionVersion; Stimulus; AnswerDefinition; Rubric; PublicationPackage; ReviewTask                                                                          | What assessable academic content exists and whether it is approved          |
| Delivery                    | PracticeSession; AssessmentDefinition/Version/Blueprint/Form/Delivery; SimulationPolicyPack/Definition/Session; Attempt; Response; Submission                              | What was delivered, under which conditions, and what the learner submitted  |
| Evaluation and intelligence | GradeRun; InstructorReview; GradeDecision; AcademicEvidence; MasteryEstimate; ReadinessModelVersion; ReadinessSnapshot; Recommendation                                     | How performance is evaluated and converted into next actions                |
| Success and outcomes        | InterventionCase; AchievementDefinition/Award; OfficialResult                                                                                                              | How support, milestones and external outcomes are managed                   |
| Operations and governance   | ItemStatistic; AcademicMetricDefinition; AcademicIntelligenceSnapshot; Announcement; NotificationDelivery; AIModelVersion; AIPromptVersion; AuditEvent; IntegrationMapping | How quality, analytics, communication, AI and compliance are operated       |

| Submission/Attempt                                | GradeDecision                                | 1 : many superseding                          | Optional until finalized                                | Exactly one effective decision per scored scope.                                  |
| GradeDecision/LearningCompletion/Practice outcome | AcademicEvidence                             | 1 : many normalized facts                     | Optional according to mapping                           | Each evidence fact traceable to source.                                           |
| PreparationJourney + Competency                   | MasteryEstimate                              | 1 : many snapshots                            | Optional until evidence                                 | One current estimate per model context.                                           |
| PreparationJourney + GoalVersion                  | ReadinessSnapshot                            | 1 : many snapshots                            | Optional/insufficient evidence allowed                  | One current snapshot per model context.                                           |
| PreparationJourney                                | Recommendation                               | 1 : many                                      | Optional                                                | Recommendations reference eligible activities.                                    |
| PreparationJourney                                | InterventionCase                             | 1 : many                                      | Optional                                                | Open duplicates controlled.                                                       |
| PreparationJourney                                | AchievementAward                             | 1 : many                                      | Optional                                                | Unique according to definition scope.                                             |
| PreparationJourney                                | OfficialResult                               | 1 : many attempts/results                     | Optional                                                | Verification and target evaluation explicit.                                      |
| Any content/submission/result                     | FileAsset                                    | many : many/one through typed references      | Optional                                                | Ownership and retention follow referencing object.                                |
| Any aggregate                                     | AuditEvent                                   | 1 : many reference                            | Conditional by audit policy                             | Audit event is append-only and not aggregate-owned.                               |

## 8.3 Composition versus reference

Composition is used only where the child cannot exist meaningfully outside its aggregate and must share transactional lifecycle—for example EnrollmentTransition under Enrollment or StudyPlanActivity under StudyPlan. Reference relationships are used for large, reused or independently versioned objects—for example QuestionVersion referencing Stimulus and Rubric.

## 8.4 Required uniqueness

At minimum, logical uniqueness applies to:

- Provider identity within authentication provider.
- One PersonProfile per canonical person identity.
- Academy code globally; academy membership per person/academy/effective period.
- RoleAssignment per membership/role/scope/effective interval where overlaps are forbidden.
- Sponsor relationship per sponsor/student/academy/relationship type/effective interval.
- Exam product code and version label/effective range.
- Competency code within framework version.
- Course code within academy; course version number within course.
- Offering code within academy; cohort code within offering.
- Active enrollment conflict key according to offering/retake policy.
- One active primary GoalVersion per ExamGoal.
- One active primary PreparationJourney per enrollment/goal context.
- Version number within each versioned lineage.
- Question item code within catalogue scope.
- Assessment form code/version within assessment version.
- Attempt number within learner/delivery scope.
- Response key within attempt/question/component.
- GradeDecision version within scored subject and one effective decision.
- AcademicEvidence source identity plus evidence type/competency to prevent duplicate projection.
- Current MasteryEstimate per journey/competency/model context.
- Current ReadinessSnapshot per journey/goal/model context.
- Recommendation deduplication key according to objective/activity/policy/active window.
- AchievementAward uniqueness according to definition scope.
- External mapping by provider/entity type/external identifier.
- Event and command idempotency identifiers.

## 8.5 Soft deletion and archival

Soft deletion is not a universal default. It is used only where business recovery is required. The preferred lifecycle verbs are cancel, revoke, retire, withdraw, supersede and archive. Hard deletion is permitted only for unused drafts or privacy-eligible data after dependency, retention and legal-hold checks. Attempts, responses, accepted submissions, grade decisions, evidence, verified official results and audit events are never ordinary soft-delete records; they are retained, invalidated or superseded.

## 8.6 Temporal history

Temporal history is required for enrollment transitions, role scopes, sponsor consent, goals, journey snapshots, paths, plans, published versions, grading decisions, evidence status, mastery, readiness, recommendations, interventions, official-result verification and audit. Effective time and recorded time must be distinguishable where late-arriving facts are possible.

# SECTION 9 — DATA DICTIONARY

This dictionary specifies logical—not physical—types. `Identifier` is an opaque stable identifier; `Code` is a governed human/business code; `StructuredDefinition` is schema-validated structured data; `RestrictedText` and `RestrictedStructuredData` require elevated protection. Physical types, lengths and index methods are determined in the physical-design stage without changing these meanings.

## 9.1 Data classification

| Class               | Examples                                                                                                                     | Minimum handling                                                                                |
| ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Public              | Published course catalogue; public academy information                                                                       | Integrity, provenance and publication controls                                                  |
| Internal            | Draft metadata; operational configuration; non-sensitive aggregate metrics                                                   | Authenticated staff access and audit for changes                                                |
| Confidential        | Profiles; enrollments; goals; progress; grades; recommendations; sponsor relationships                                       | Encryption, scoped authorization, RLS, controlled exports and retention                         |
| Restricted academic | Answer definitions; protected question pools; essays; recordings; detailed evidence; AI prompts containing protected context | Need-to-know access, private storage, short-lived delivery, stronger audit and no ordinary logs |
| Restricted security | Credentials, tokens, provider secrets, MFA recovery material, security incidents                                             | Secret management, no application-table exposure, least privilege, rotation and security audit  |

## 9.2 Logical index rule

An index is justified by a named access pattern, not by every foreign key or field automatically. Phase 0.5 defines logical needs; Phase 1 physical design must validate column order, selectivity, update cost, partial conditions and query plans. High-priority patterns are active entitlement lookup, journey/workspace resolution, form/attempt loading, response autosave, evidence/mastery/readiness lookup, open recommendations/interventions, publishing queues, audit investigations and external mapping.

# SECTION 10 — PERMISSION MATRIX

Authorization combines role-based access with academy, course/offering assignment, enrollment/journey ownership, content state, consent and operation attributes.

**Codes:** C Create; R Read; U Update; D Delete unused draft only unless stated; A Approve; P Publish; O Override; V Review. “Scoped” means academy/course/offering/journey/assignment and lifecycle checks still apply. A dash means no permission.

## 10.1 Separation of duties

1. Content Author and final approver should be different actors for Tier 3–4 academic assets.
2. Instructor does not automatically receive question-answer, platform configuration or cross-course access.
3. Student Success Officer receives intervention and approved academic summaries, not unrestricted answer keys or private AI conversations.
4. Administrator is academy-scoped; Super Administrator is platform-scoped.
5. Sponsor is a delegated-view relationship, not an ordinary staff or student role.
6. Override is independent of update; grade, timer and security overrides require dedicated permission.
7. A role assignment may be time-limited and narrowed to course, offering or cohort.
8. Effective permissions are the intersection of role, scope, relationship, state, consent, field policy and command rule.

## 10.2 Permission evaluation order

```text
Authenticated principal
→ active account
→ active academy membership or student/sponsor relationship
→ role capability
→ resource academy
→ course/offering/instructor assignment or enrollment/journey ownership
→ lifecycle state
→ field-level and consent policy
→ command-specific guard
```

# SECTION 11 — ROW-LEVEL SECURITY MATRIX

RLS is defence in depth and defaults to deny. Public or safe catalogue views are explicit exceptions. Trusted workers receive narrowly scoped service identities; they do not receive an unrestricted application-wide bypass by default.

| Entity / entity group                                                          | Who can view                                                                                                                 | Who can update                                                                                               | Who can delete                                                                          | Who can approve                                                            | Who can archive                               | Rationale                                                                                                |

| PublicationPackage; ReviewTask                                                 | Authors see own candidates/tasks; reviewers assigned gates; admins scope-wide                                                | Assigned actor according to gate; immutable decisions                                                        | Unused draft package/task only                                                          | Named reviewer/approver according to tier                                  | Administrator                                 | Separation of duties and immutable approvals must be enforced.                                           |
